local Players = game:GetService("Players") local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") local LogService = game:GetService("LogService") local RunService = game:GetService("RunService") local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer local PlayerScripts = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts") local KILL_NAMES = { "Security","DeviceChecker","AntiCheatDetector","ClientDetector", "IntegrityVerifier","CheatDetector","ViolationReport","FlagSystem", "MovementValidator","RemoteVerifier","HookDetector","SynapseDetector" } pcall(function() for _, n in ipairs(KILL_NAMES) do pcall(function() for _, r in ipairs(RS:GetDescendants()) do if r.Name == n then r:Destroy() end end end) pcall(function() for _, r in ipairs(PlayerScripts:GetDescendants()) do if r.Name == n then r:Destroy() end end end) end end) local BLOCK_NAME = { -- banit_events (24) kick=true, ban=true, unban=true, timed_ban=true, server_ban=true, server_unban=true, global_ban=true, global_timed_ban=true, jail=true, server_lock=true, server_shutdown=true, announce=true, apply_effect=true, clear_effects=true, delete_effect=true, clear_tools=true, delete_tool=true, give_tool=true, visibility=true, ClientValidation=true, } -- blocklist by ancestor path (covers anything rooted in exe_storage / banit_events / custom_command_events / webhook_events) local ANCESTOR_BLOCK = { exe_storage=true, banit_events=true, custom_command_events=true, webhook_events=true, } -- these MUST keep flowing through or the server times us out / freezes us local REMOTE_KEEP = { Ping=true, MenuState=true, FocusState=true, Fps=true, LibMPDevice=true, LibMPRequiring=true, LibMPRequired=true, LibMPFrame=true, LiveRequest=true, LiveResponse=true, EventExportClientMetrics=true, EventClientInfoChanged=true, } local function ancestor_blocked(inst) local cur = inst while cur and cur ~= game do if ANCESTOR_BLOCK[cur.Name] then return true end cur = cur.Parent end return false end local function remote_blocked(inst) if REMOTE_KEEP[inst.Name] then return false end if BLOCK_NAME[inst.Name] then return true end return ancestor_blocked(inst) end local function strHasXcode(v) if typeof(v) ~= "string" then return false end if v:find("[Xx]%s*[%-%_%.%:]%s*%d%d?%d?") then return true end if #v >= 24 and v:match("^[A-Za-z0-9%+/_%-]+=?=?$") then return true end if (#v == 32 or #v == 40 or #v == 64) and v:match("^[0-9a-fA-F]+$") then return true end return false end local function scanDeep(v, depth) depth = depth or 0 if depth > 8 then return false end local t = typeof(v) if t == "string" then return strHasXcode(v) elseif t == "table" then local arr, n = true, 0 for k, vd in pairs(v) do if type(k) == "number" and type(vd) == "number" and vd >= 0 and vd <= 255 and vd % 1 == 0 then n = n + 1 else arr = false; break end end if arr and n >= 4 and n <= 256 then local ok, s = pcall(function() local b = {} for i = 1, n do b[i] = string.char(v[i] or 0) end return table.concat(b) end) if ok and strHasXcode(s) then return true end end for k, vd in pairs(v) do if type(k) == "string" and strHasXcode(k) then return true end if scanDeep(vd, depth + 1) then return true end end end return false end local function argsContainXcode(args) for i = 1, #args do if scanDeep(args[i]) then return true end end return false end local function hookMethod(className, methodName, isInvoke) pcall(function() local dummy = Instance.new(className) local m = dummy[methodName] local old old = hookfunction(m, newcclosure(function(self, ...) local args = {...} if remote_blocked(self) or argsContainXcode(args) then if isInvoke then return nil end return end return old(self, table.unpack(args)) end)) dummy:Destroy() end) end hookMethod("RemoteEvent", "FireServer", false) hookMethod("UnreliableRemoteEvent","FireServer", false) hookMethod("RemoteFunction", "InvokeServer", true) pcall(function() if typeof(hookmetamethod) == "function" then local oldNamecall oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(self, ...) local args = {...} local method = getnamecallmethod and getnamecallmethod() or "" if method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer" then if remote_blocked(self) or argsContainXcode(args) then if method == "InvokeServer" then return nil end return end end return oldNamecall(self, table.unpack(args)) end)) end end) pcall(function() local function clean(msg) msg = tostring(msg):lower() for _, sig in ipairs({"synapse","krnl","scriptware","fluxus","hydrogen","electron", "executor","inject","opencode","hookfunction","hookmetamethod","newcclosure", "getrawmetatable","getgenv","getrenv","loadstring","ac killer","ac bypass"}) do if msg:find(sig) then return true end end return false end local orig = LogService.GetLogHistory hookfunction(orig, newcclosure(function(self, ...) local raw = orig(self, ...) local out = {} for _, e in ipairs(raw) do if not (e.message and clean(e.message)) then out[#out + 1] = e end end return out end)) end) task.spawn(function() local exe = RS:WaitForChild("exe_storage", 5) local cfg = exe and exe:WaitForChild("configuration", 5) local prof = cfg and cfg:WaitForChild("profile", 5) local wmin, wmax, jmin, jmax = 14, 50, 50, 60 if prof then pcall(function() wmin = prof:WaitForChild("walkspeed_min", 3).Value or wmin wmax = prof:WaitForChild("walkspeed_max", 3).Value or wmax jmin = prof:WaitForChild("jump_min", 3).Value or jmin jmax = prof:WaitForChild("jump_max", 3).Value or jmax prof.walkspeed_min.Changed:Connect(function(v) wmin = v end) prof.walkspeed_max.Changed:Connect(function(v) wmax = v end) prof.jump_min.Changed:Connect(function(v) jmin = v end) prof.jump_max.Changed:Connect(function(v) jmax = v end) end) end local function clampHum(hum) if not hum then return end pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(hum.WalkSpeed, wmin, wmax) hum.JumpPower = math.clamp(hum.JumpPower, jmin, jmax) hum.JumpHeight = math.clamp(hum.JumpHeight, 0, (jmax / 10)) hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function() if hum.WalkSpeed > wmax or hum.WalkSpeed < wmin then hum.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(hum.WalkSpeed, wmin, wmax) end end) hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower"):Connect(function() if hum.JumpPower > jmax or hum.JumpPower < jmin then hum.JumpPower = math.clamp(hum.JumpPower, jmin, jmax) end end) end) end local function attach(char) local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) if hum then clampHum(hum) end end if LocalPlayer.Character then attach(LocalPlayer.Character) end LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(attach) end) pcall(function() local ee = RS:WaitForChild("exe_storage", 5) if not ee then return end local be = ee:WaitForChild("events", 5) and ee.events:WaitForChild("banit_events", 5) if not be then return end for _, n in ipairs({"notify","announce","visibility","server_lock","kick","ban","jail"})
